# KTM 450 SX-F 2024: aftermarket parts

5211 listings whose stated fitment names this bike: 523 researched parts (with what they change and rider findings), 1145 dealer and retailer listings specific to this bike, and 3543 universal retailer listings (oil, grips, batteries and the like, counted below; every listing is in [parts.csv](../parts.csv)). Research candidates: check fitment on the maker's page before relying on a row. Back to [the family catalog](../CATALOG.md).
